Result: 建立了自适应采样下单索引估计量的渐近正态性（提供有效置信区域）和RKHS估计量的方向泛函中心极限定理（提供渐近有效点态置信区间）。获得了有限时间遗憾保证，包括在常见链接Lipschitz条件下的Õ(√T)速率。

Conclusion: 该研究为单索引上下文赌博机中的同时学习和推断提供了统一框架，表明可以利用半参数结构而不牺牲统计效率。新开发的分析工具适用于自适应收集数据的推断问题。

Abstract: We study contextual bandits with finitely many actions in which the reward of each arm follows a single-index model with an arm-specific index parameter and an unknown nonparametric link function. We consider a regime in which arms correspond to stable decision options and covariates evolve adaptively under the bandit policy. This setting creates significant statistical challenges: the sampling distribution depends on the allocation rule, observations are dependent over time, and inverse-propensity weighting induces variance inflation. We propose a kernelized $\varepsilon$-greedy algorithm that combines Stein-based estimation of the index parameters with inverse-propensity-weighted kernel ridge regression for the reward functions. This approach enables flexible semiparametric learning while retaining interpretability. Our analysis develops new tools for inference with adaptively collected data. We establish asymptotic normality for the single-index estimator under adaptive sampling, yielding valid confidence regions, and derive a directional functional central limit theorem for the RKHS estimator, which provides asymptotically valid pointwise confidence intervals. The analysis relies on concentration bounds for inverse-weighted Gram matrices together with martingale central limit theorems. We further obtain finite-time regret guarantees, including $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ rates under common-link Lipschitz conditions, showing that semiparametric structure can be exploited without sacrificing statistical efficiency. These results provide a unified framework for simultaneous learning and inference in single-index contextual bandits.

</details>


### [184] [Starting Off on the Wrong Foot: Pitfalls in Data Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18190)
*Jiayi Guo,Panyi Dong,Zhiyu Quan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出针对保险损失数据的新型数据准备框架，结合支持点分割和Chatterjee相关系数，提升模型稳健性和计算效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据准备方法（如随机训练-测试划分）在处理高度不平衡的保险损失数据时，会产生不可靠和不稳定的结果，影响下游建模的统计有效性和可靠性

Method: 提出统一的数据准备框架，整合两个统计进展：1) 使用支持点进行代表性数据分割，确保分区间的分布一致性；2) 使用Chatterjee相关系数进行初始非参数特征筛选，捕捉特征相关性和依赖结构。该框架还包含缺失数据处理，并嵌入到自定义的InsurAutoML管道中

Result: 在模拟数据集和学术文献常用数据集上的评估表明，该方法显著增强了模型稳健性和可解释性，同时大幅减少了不同保险损失建模任务的计算资源需求

Conclusion: 该研究为高风险保险应用提供了关键的方法论升级，证明采用统计严谨的数据准备方法对于获得可靠结果至关重要

Abstract: When working with real-world insurance data, practitioners often encounter challenges during the data preparation stage that can undermine the statistical validity and reliability of downstream modeling. This study illustrates that conventional data preparation procedures such as random train-test partitioning, often yield unreliable and unstable results when confronted with highly imbalanced insurance loss data. To mitigate these limitations, we propose a novel data preparation framework leveraging two recent statistical advancements: support points for representative data splitting to ensure distributional consistency across partitions, and the Chatterjee correlation coefficient for initial, non-parametric feature screening to capture feature relevance and dependence structure. We further integrate these theoretical advances into a unified, efficient framework that also incorporates missing-data handling, and embed this framework within our custom InsurAutoML pipeline. The performance of the proposed approach is evaluated using both simulated datasets and datasets often cited in the academic literature. Our findings definitively demonstrate that incorporating statistically rigorous data preparation methods not only significantly enhances model robustness and interpretability but also substantially reduces computational resource requirements across diverse insurance loss modeling tasks. This work provides a crucial methodological upgrade for achieving reliable results in high stakes insurance applications.

</details>


### [185] [Multi-Domain Causal Empirical Bayes Under Linear Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18404)
*Bohan Wu,Julius von Kügelgen,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于经验贝叶斯的因果表示学习方法，通过多领域数据利用不变结构改进因果变量估计质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表示学习中的估计问题研究较少，而多领域数据自然构成了经验贝叶斯方法适合处理的同时推断问题。

Method: 提出基于经验贝叶斯的f建模算法，采用线性测量模型和源自共享无环SCM的干预先验，当图和干预目标已知时，开发基于因果结构得分匹配的EM风格算法。

Result: 在合成数据实验中，该方法相比其他因果表示学习方法实现了更准确的估计。

Conclusion: 经验贝叶斯方法能有效利用多领域数据中的不变结构，提升因果表示学习的估计质量。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) aims to learn low-dimensional causal latent variables from high-dimensional observations. While identifiability has been extensively studied for CRL, estimation has been less explored. In this paper, we explore the use of empirical Bayes (EB) to estimate causal representations. In particular, we consider the problem of learning from data from multiple domains, where differences between domains are modeled by interventions in a shared underlying causal model. Multi-domain CRL naturally poses a simultaneous inference problem that EB is designed to tackle. Here, we propose an EB $f$-modeling algorithm that improves the quality of learned causal variables by exploiting invariant structure within and across domains. Specifically, we consider a linear measurement model and interventional priors arising from a shared acyclic SCM. When the graph and intervention targets are known, we develop an EM-style algorithm based on causally structured score matching. We further discuss EB $\rmg$-modeling in the context of existing CRL approaches. In experiments on synthetic data, our proposed method achieves more accurate estimation than other methods for CRL.

</details>


### [186] [Statistical Testing Framework for Clustering Pipelines by Selective Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18413)
*Yugo Miyata,Tomohiro Shiraishi,Shunichi Nishino,Ichiro Takeuchi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于选择性推断的统计检验框架，用于评估数据依赖型聚类分析管道结果的统计可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中，分析结果通常需要经过多个数据依赖步骤的管道处理，但缺乏量化这些管道输出结果统计可靠性的方法

Method: 基于选择性推断的统计检验框架，针对包含异常检测、特征选择和聚类等预定义组件的聚类管道，系统构建有效的统计检验

Result: 理论证明该检验能在任意名义水平上控制I类错误率，在合成和真实数据集上的实验验证了其有效性和正确性

Conclusion: 提出的选择性推断框架为评估数据依赖型分析管道结果的统计显著性提供了系统方法，特别适用于聚类分析管道

Abstract: A data analysis pipeline is a structured sequence of steps that transforms raw data into meaningful insights by integrating multiple analysis algorithms.In many practical applications, analytical findings are obtained only after data pass through several data-dependent procedures within such pipelines.In this study, we address the problem of quantifying the statistical reliability of results produced by data analysis pipelines.As a proof of concept, we focus on clustering pipelines that identify cluster structures from complex and heterogeneous data through procedures such as outlier detection, feature selection, and clustering.We propose a novel statistical testing framework to assess the significance of clustering results obtained through these pipelines.Our framework, based on selective inference, enables the systematic construction of valid statistical tests for clustering pipelines composed of predefined components.We prove that the proposed test controls the type I error rate at any nominal level and demonstrate its validity and effectiveness through experiments on synthetic and real datasets.

</details>


### [187] [Precise Performance of Linear Denoisers in the Proportional Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18483)
*Reza Ghane,Danil Akhtiamov,Babak Hassibi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究线性去噪器在未知协方差数据加噪声情况下的性能，提出通过注入不同协方差的合成噪声来训练线性去噪器的方法，使用CGMT理论分析泛化误差，数值模拟显示该方法优于经验维纳滤波器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据协方差未知且无法使用标准维纳滤波器的情况下，受扩散模型去噪步骤启发，研究如何直接从数据中训练线性去噪器，而不是先估计协方差再构建经验维纳滤波器

Method: 通过注入协方差为Σ₁（不同于真实噪声协方差Σ_z）的高斯噪声来合成含噪样本，然后使用最小二乘法训练线性去噪器W，使其近似原始数据；在比例极限n/d→κ>1下，应用凸高斯最小最大定理（CGMT）推导泛化误差的闭式表达式

Result: 数值模拟表明，该方法在许多场景下优于经验维纳滤波器，并且当κ→∞时，性能接近最优维纳滤波器；通过优化Σ₁可以获得最佳去噪器

Conclusion: 提出了一种直接从数据训练线性去噪器的有效方法，该方法在协方差未知情况下优于传统经验维纳滤波器，为扩散模型等应用中的去噪问题提供了理论分析和实用解决方案

Abstract: In the present paper we study the performance of linear denoisers for noisy data of the form $\mathbf{x} + \mathbf{z}$, where $\mathbf{x} \in \mathbb{R}^d$ is the desired data with zero mean and unknown covariance $\mathbfΣ$, and $\mathbf{z} \sim \mathcal{N}(0, \mathbfΣ_{\mathbf{z}})$ is additive noise. Since the covariance $\mathbfΣ$ is not known, the standard Wiener filter cannot be employed for denoising. Instead we assume we are given samples $\mathbf{x}_1,\dots,\mathbf{x}_n \in \mathbb{R}^d$ from the true distribution. A standard approach would then be to estimate $\mathbfΣ$ from the samples and use it to construct an ``empirical" Wiener filter. However, in this paper, motivated by the denoising step in diffusion models, we take a different approach whereby we train a linear denoiser $\mathbf{W}$ from the data itself. In particular, we synthetically construct noisy samples $\hat{\mathbf{x}}_i$ of the data by injecting the samples with Gaussian noise with covariance $\mathbfΣ_1 \neq \mathbfΣ_{\mathbf{z}}$ and find the best $\mathbf{W}$ that approximates $\mathbf{W}\hat{\mathbf{x}}_i \approx \mathbf{x}_i$ in a least-squares sense. In the proportional regime $\frac{n}{d} \rightarrow κ> 1$ we use the {\it Convex Gaussian Min-Max Theorem (CGMT)} to analytically find the closed form expression for the generalization error of the denoiser obtained from this process. Using this expression one can optimize over $\mathbfΣ_1$ to find the best possible denoiser. Our numerical simulations show that our denoiser outperforms the ``empirical" Wiener filter in many scenarios and approaches the optimal Wiener filter as $κ\rightarrow\infty$.

</details>


### [188] [On the Peril of (Even a Little) Nonstationarity in Satisficing Regret Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18514)
*Yixuan Zhang,Ruihao Zhu,Qiaomin Xie*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究了非平稳K臂老虎机中的满意后悔保证，发现在L≥2个平稳段的一般可实现、分段平稳设置中，最优后悔为Θ(LlogT)，与L=1时的常数后悔形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受决策理论中"满意原则"的启发，研究非平稳K臂老虎机的满意后悔保证，探索非平稳性对后悔界的影响。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的基于Fano的框架，通过"后交互参考"构造专门针对非平稳老虎机，扩展了经典被动估计的Fano方法和最近用于平稳老虎机的交互式Fano技术。

Result: 在L≥2个平稳段的一般可实现、分段平稳设置中，最优后悔为Θ(LlogT)，与L=1时的常数后悔形成鲜明对比，表明即使只有少量非平稳性，最优后悔也必须随T增长。

Conclusion: 非平稳性显著改变了满意后悔的保证，从平稳设置中的常数后悔变为非平稳设置中的对数增长后悔。论文还讨论了在某些特殊机制下常数后悔仍然可能的情况。

Abstract: Motivated by the principle of satisficing in decision-making, we study satisficing regret guarantees for nonstationary $K$-armed bandits. We show that in the general realizable, piecewise-stationary setting with $L$ stationary segments, the optimal regret is $Θ(L\log T)$ as long as $L\geq 2$. This stands in sharp contrast to the case of $L=1$ (i.e., the stationary setting), where a $T$-independent $Θ(1)$ satisficing regret is achievable under realizability. In other words, the optimal regret has to scale with $T$ even if just a little nonstationarity presents. A key ingredient in our analysis is a novel Fano-based framework tailored to nonstationary bandits via a \emph{post-interaction reference} construction. This framework strictly extends the classical Fano method for passive estimation as well as recent interactive Fano techniques for stationary bandits. As a complement, we also discuss a special regime in which constant satisficing regret is again possible.

</details>


### [189] [A Theoretical Comparison of No-U-Turn Sampler Variants: Necessary and Su?cient Convergence Conditions and Mixing Time Analysis under Gaussian Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18640)
*Samuel Gruffaz,Kyurae Kim,Fares Guehtar,Hadrien Duval-decaix,Pacôme Trautmann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文首次为NUTS的两个主要变体(NUTS-mul和NUTS-BPS)建立了几何遍历性的必要条件和充分条件，并获得了NUTS-BPS在标准高斯分布上的首个混合时间结果，揭示了两种变体在定性行为相似但定量收敛速率存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NUTS是现代贝叶斯软件库的核心计算工具，但其两个主要变体(NUTS-mul使用多项采样，NUTS-BPS使用偏置渐进采样)的理论比较存在显著空白，需要建立收敛性保证的理论基础。

Method: 通过理论分析推导：1) 为两种NUTS变体建立几何遍历性的必要条件；2) 为NUTS-mul建立几何遍历性和遍历性的充分条件；3) 在标准高斯分布上获得NUTS-BPS的首个混合时间结果。

Result: 结果显示：1) NUTS-mul和NUTS-BPS在定性行为上几乎相同，几何遍历性取决于目标分布的尾部特性；2) 在典型高斯测度初始化下，两种方法的混合时间都按O(d^{1/4})缩放(含对数因子)；3) NUTS-BPS的相关常数严格小于NUTS-mul。

Conclusion: 本文填补了NUTS两种主要变体理论比较的空白，首次建立了系统的收敛性理论框架，为实际应用中的算法选择提供了理论依据，表明虽然两种方法定性相似，但NUTS-BPS具有更优的定量收敛性能。

Abstract: The No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS) is the computational workhorse of modern Bayesian software libraries, yet its qualitative and quantitative convergence guarantees were established only recently. A significant gap remains in the theoretical comparison of its two main variants: NUTS-mul and NUTS-BPS, which use multinomial sampling and biased progressive sampling, respectively, for index selection. In this paper, we address this gap in three contributions. First, we derive the first necessary conditions for geometric ergodicity for both variants. Second, we establish the first sufficient conditions for geometric ergodicity and ergodicity for NUTS-mul. Third, we obtain the first mixing time result for NUTS-BPS on a standard Gaussian distribution. Our results show that NUTS-mul and NUTS-BPS exhibit nearly identical qualitative behavior, with geometric ergodicity depending on the tail properties of the target distribution. However, they differ quantitatively in their convergence rates. More precisely, when initialized in the typical set of the canonical Gaussian measure, the mixing times of both NUTS-mul and NUTS-BPS scale as $O(d^{1/4})$ up to logarithmic factors, where $d$ denotes the dimension. Nevertheless, the associated constants are strictly smaller for NUTS-BPS.

</details>


### [190] [SRRM: Improving Recursive Transport Surrogates in the Small-Discrepancy Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18781)
*Yufei Zhang,Tao Wang,Jingyi Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文研究了递归划分方法作为Wasserstein距离的替代度量，分析了其统计行为和小差异区域的分辨率问题，提出了选择性递归秩匹配方法以改善分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归划分方法为Wasserstein距离提供了计算高效的替代度量，但其统计行为和小差异区域的分辨率尚未得到充分理解。需要研究这类方法的理论性质并改进其在小差异区域的分辨能力。

Method: 研究递归秩匹配作为这类方法的代表性实例，在基于总体锚定的参考框架下进行分析。首先建立锚定经验RRM在二次成本下的收敛性，识别导致小差异区域分辨率损失的主导失配机制，然后提出选择性递归秩匹配来抑制这些主导失配。

Result: 建立了锚定经验RRM的一致性和显式收敛速率，识别了小差异区域分辨率损失的主导失配机制，提出的SRRM方法在适度增加计算成本的情况下提供了对Wasserstein距离更高保真度的实用替代度量。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和机制识别，提出的选择性递归秩匹配方法有效改善了递归划分方法在小差异区域的分辨率问题，为Wasserstein距离提供了更精确的计算高效替代度量。

Abstract: Recursive partitioning methods provide computationally efficient surrogates for the Wasserstein distance, yet their statistical behavior and their resolution in the small-discrepancy regime remain insufficiently understood. We study Recursive Rank Matching (RRM) as a representative instance of this class under a population-anchored reference. In this setting, we establish consistency and an explicit convergence rate for the anchored empirical RRM under the quadratic cost. We then identify a dominant mismatch mechanism responsible for the loss of resolution in the small-discrepancy regime. Based on this analysis, we introduce Selective Recursive Rank Matching (SRRM), which suppresses the resulting dominant mismatches and yields a higher-fidelity practical surrogate for the Wasserstein distance at moderate additional computational cost.

</details>


### [191] [Unified Taxonomy for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18941)
*Bruna Alves,Armando J. Pinho,Sónia Gouveia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个针对多变量时间序列异常检测（MTSAD）深度学习方法的统一分类法，包含输入、输出和模型三个部分的11个维度，通过分析现有方法并验证于最新文献，揭示了Transformer、重构和预测模型的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列异常检测领域发展迅速但缺乏系统性分类，现有研究碎片化，需要统一的分类框架来整合知识并为未来研究提供参考。

Method: 采用双重方法：首先通过对方法论研究的全面分析推导维度，其次整合综述论文的见解，最后用最新出版物验证分类法。

Result: 揭示了MTSAD领域向Transformer架构以及重构和预测模型收敛的趋势，为自适应和生成式趋势奠定了基础，验证了分类法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的统一分类法能够适应未来发展，整合了碎片化知识，为MTSAD领域提供了系统化的参考框架，支持新类别和维度的扩展。

Abstract: The topic of Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection (MTSAD) has grown rapidly over the past years, with a steady rise in publications and Deep Learning (DL) models becoming the dominant paradigm. To address the lack of systematization in the field, this study introduces a novel and unified taxonomy with eleven dimensions over three parts (Input, Output and Model) for the categorization of DL-based MTSAD methods. The dimensions were established in a two-fold approach. First, they derived from a comprehensive analysis of methodological studies. Second, insights from review papers were incorporated. Furthermore, the proposed taxonomy was validated using an additional set of recent publications, providing a clear overview of methodological trends in MTSAD. Results reveal a convergence toward Transformer-based and reconstruction and prediction models, setting the foundation for emerging adaptive and generative trends. Building on and complementing existing surveys, this unified taxonomy is designed to accommodate future developments, allowing for new categories or dimensions to be added as the field progresses. This work thus consolidates fragmented knowledge in the field and provides a reference point for future research in MTSAD.

</details>


### [192] [The Exponentially Weighted Signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19198)
*Alexandre Bloch,Samuel N. Cohen,Terry Lyons,Joël Mouterde,Benjamin Walker*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出了指数加权签名（EWS），通过引入有界线性算子扩展了传统签名方法，使其能够对历史信息进行上下文相关的加权处理，同时保留了经典签名的代数优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统签名方法在处理多维路径时，对所有历史信息采用统一处理方式，缺乏对过去信息相关性的上下文感知机制。这限制了签名在需要区分历史信息重要性场景中的应用能力。

Method: 引入指数加权签名（EWS），将指数衰减记忆（EFM）签名从对角算子推广到一般有界线性算子。这些算子允许在时间加权层面进行跨通道耦合，并支持更丰富的记忆动态（如振荡、增长和状态依赖行为）。EWS被证明是张量代数上线性控制微分方程的唯一解，并推广了状态空间模型以及路径的拉普拉斯和傅里叶变换。

Result: EWS保持了类群结构，支持高效计算和基于梯度的学习。通过两个基于SDE的回归任务，实证证明了EWS在表达能力上优于传统签名和EFM签名。

Conclusion: EWS通过引入灵活的加权算子，成功解决了传统签名方法缺乏上下文感知的问题，在保留经典签名代数优势的同时，显著提升了表达能力，为路径分析提供了更强大的数学框架。

Abstract: The signature is a canonical representation of a multidimensional path over an interval. However, it treats all historical information uniformly, offering no intrinsic mechanism for contextualising the relevance of the past. To address this, we introduce the Exponentially Weighted Signature (EWS), generalising the Exponentially Fading Memory (EFM) signature from diagonal to general bounded linear operators. These operators enable cross-channel coupling at the level of temporal weighting together with richer memory dynamics including oscillatory, growth, and regime-dependent behaviour, while preserving the algebraic strengths of the classical signature. We show that the EWS is the unique solution to a linear controlled differential equation on the tensor algebra, and that it generalises both state-space models and the Laplace and Fourier transforms of the path. The group-like structure of the EWS enables efficient computation and makes the framework amenable to gradient-based learning, with the full semigroup action parametrised by and learned through its generator. We use this framework to empirically demonstrate the expressivity gap between the EWS and both the signature and EFM on two SDE-based regression tasks.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Highly Adaptive Empirical Risk Minimization with Principal Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18204)
*Carlos García Meixide,Mingxun Wang,Alejandro Schuler,Mark J. van der Laan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: PC-HA方法通过主成分分析为HAL提供理论有效的降维，解决HAL计算复杂度指数增长问题，同时保持理论保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAL（高度自适应套索）在非参数最小损失估计中具有前所未有的理论保证，但在中高维度下，其指数增长的指示器基展开导致计算不可行。现有降维策略缺乏理论依据，需要一种既保持理论保证又降低计算复杂度的新方法。

Method: 提出主成分高度自适应（PC-HA）估计器家族，通过主成分分析对HAL的基函数进行降维。建立PC-HA估计器求解的得分方程的形式化结果，使得能够将HAL的插件效率和逐点渐近正态性结果转移到PC-HA估计器。

Result: PC-HA方法首次为HAL提供了原则性且理论有效的降维方案。在可比较的复杂度控制下，能够将HAL的理论保证（包括插件效率和逐点渐近正态性）转移到PC-HA估计器。

Conclusion: PC-HA方法解决了HAL在实际应用中的计算瓶颈，为高维非参数估计提供了理论可靠且计算可行的新框架，填补了现有降维策略缺乏理论依据的空白。

Abstract: The Highly Adaptive Lasso (HAL) delivers unprecedented guarantees in nonparametric minimum loss estimation under minimal smoothness assumptions, such as dimension-free minimax optimal rates. However, the practical use of HAL has been severely limited by its exponentially growing computationally prohibitive indicator basis expansion in moderate to high dimensions. Existing screening strategies drastically reduce this dimension but lack any theoretical justification. We introduce the Principal Component Highly Adaptive (PC-HA) family of estimators, which for the first time provide a principled and theoretically valid dimension reduction. We establish formal results on the score equations solved by these PC-HA estimators, allowing to transfer plug-in efficiency and pointwise asymptotic normality results from HAL to these PC-HA estimators, under comparable complexity control.

</details>


### [194] [Minimax Optimal Estimation of Mean and Covariance Functions with Spectral Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18311)
*Naveen Gupta,Bharath K Sriperumbudur*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出基于再生核希尔伯特空间的正则化框架，用于估计离散观测函数数据的均值和协方差函数，无需目标函数属于RKHS，建立了收敛速率和极小极大下界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数数据分析中，均值和协方差函数的估计是基本问题，特别是对于离散观测的函数数据。现有方法通常要求目标函数属于底层RKHS，这限制了应用范围。

Method: 在RKHS设置下构建正则化框架，采用谱正则化技术，在Hölder型源条件下工作，允许广泛的正则化方案，适应目标函数的各种光滑性假设

Result: 推导了所提估计量的收敛速率，通过获得匹配的极小极大下界建立了最优性，证明方法在统计意义上是最优的

Conclusion: 提出的正则化框架为函数数据的均值和协方差估计提供了灵活且最优的方法，放宽了目标函数必须属于RKHS的限制，扩展了方法的适用范围

Abstract: Estimation of the mean and covariance functions is a fundamental problem in functional data analysis, particularly for discretely observed functional data. In this work, we study a regularization-based framework for estimating the mean and the covariance functions within a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) setting. Our approach utilizes a spectral regularization technique under Hölder-type source conditions, allowing for a broad class of regularization schemes and accommodating a wide range of smoothness assumptions on the target functions. Unlike previous works in the literature, the proposed work does not require the target functions to belong to the underlying RKHS. Convergence rates for the proposed estimators are derived, and optimality is established by obtaining matching minimax lower bounds.

</details>


### [195] [Approximation by mixtures of multivariate Erlang distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18506)
*Hien Duy Nguyen*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 证明了具有共同率参数的多变量Erlang混合密度在L^p空间中是稠密的，并给出了多种定量逼近结果和收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多变量Erlang混合分布在概率密度函数逼近中的理论性质，特别是建立其稠密性结果和定量逼近速率，以加强弱逼近理论并为实际应用提供理论保证。

Method: 采用构造性方法：使用一维Szász-Mirakjan-Kantorovich算子获得Erlang混合逼近，然后通过张量积构造具有共同尺度的多变量逼近。在此基础上推导各种定量结果。

Result: 证明了有限多变量Erlang混合密度在L^p空间中的稠密性；获得了紧集一致逼近界、局部Hölder条件下的收敛速率（n^{-α/2}）、加权sup范数收敛、加权和非加权L^p速率，以及基于混合分量数量的显式速率。

Conclusion: 多变量Erlang混合分布具有强大的逼近能力，不仅理论上稠密，而且可获得多种定量逼近速率，这加强了多变量Erlang混合分布的弱逼近理论，并可直接推广到更广泛的分布类如乘积-gamma混合。

Abstract: We prove that finite multivariate Erlang mixture densities with a common rate parameter are dense in the class of probability densities on $\mathbb{R}_{+}^{d}$ that belong to $L^{p}$, for every dimension $d\in\mathbb{N}$ and every $1\le p<\infty$. The argument is constructive: the one-dimensional Szász--Mirakjan--Kantorovich operator yields Erlang mixture approximations, and its tensor product yields multivariate approximants with a common scale. We then obtain several quantitative consequences. These include compact-set uniform approximation bounds and, under local Hölder conditions of order $α\in(0,1]$, rates of order $n^{-α/2}$ as the common scale $1/n$ tends to zero, whole-domain convergence in weighted sup norms, weighted and unweighted $L^{p}$ rates, and explicit rates for finite mixtures indexed by the number of mixture components. In particular, if the approximating density is required to have at most $K$ mixture components, then on fixed compact cubes we obtain an algebraic rate of order $K^{-α/(2d)}$; in global weighted sup norms we obtain the explicit algebraic component-count rate $K^{-α/[2d(2d+α)]}$; and for $1<p<\infty$ we obtain corresponding weighted $L^{p}$ component-count rates. The results strengthen the weak-approximation theory for multivariate Erlang mixture distributions and yield immediate corollaries for broader classes such as product-gamma mixtures.  \noindent\textbf{Keywords:} multivariate Erlang mixtures; Erlang distributions; Szász--Mirakjan--Kantorovich operator; density approximation; weighted $L^{p}$ approximation; approximation rates.

</details>


### [196] [Sometimes nonparametrics beat parametrics, even when the model is right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18590)
*Morten Byholt,Nils Lid Hjort*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 核密度估计器在小样本情况下，即使参数模型正确，也能在均方误差上优于参数密度估计器，挑战了传统统计假设与性能关系的认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在挑战统计学中关于建模假设与推断性能关系的传统认知。传统观点认为，更强的假设（如果成立）会带来更好的统计性能，而较少假设的方法虽然更稳健，但在特定条件下不如专门方法。作者发现这一观点并非普遍成立。

Method: 通过密度估计的案例研究，对比参数和非参数方法。主要使用简单的核密度估计器，在参数模型正确的情况下（特别是正态密度估计），分析其在小样本条件下的性能。研究涉及精确的小样本密度估计器分析。

Result: 研究发现，在小样本情况下，简单的核密度估计器在均方积分误差（MISE）上能够优于直接估计的参数密度估计器，即使是在参数模型的"主场"（即参数模型正确的情况下）。这一结果挑战了传统认知。

Conclusion: 统计假设与推断性能的关系并非普遍成立。在某些情况下，非参数方法即使在参数模型正确的小样本条件下也能表现更优。这一发现对统计教学和实践具有重要意义，提示我们需要更细致地理解不同方法在不同条件下的性能表现。

Abstract: A basic issue in both teaching of and practice of statistics is the interplay between modelling assumptions and inference performance. The general message conveyed is that stronger assumptions lead to better statistical performance of the relevant estimators, tests and confidence intervals, provided that these assumptions hold. On the other hand, fewer assumptions often lead to safer and more robust methods that are good also outside narrow conditions, but not quite as good as specialist methods that exploit such narrower conditions, if these are fulfilled.
  This interplay is nicely illustrated in the context of density estimation, where parametric and nonparametric methods can be contrasted. The parametric ones have mean squared errors of size $O(n^{-1})$ in terms of sample size $n$ if the parametric model is right, but are not even consistent outside the model. The nonparametric methods are everywhere consistent and have mean squared errors of size $O(n^{-4/5})$ for broad classes of estimands.
  The point we are making here is that this picture is not universally true! We show that a simple kernel density estimator can perform better than a directly estimated parametric density on the latter's home turf, for small sample sizes, in the sense of mean integrated squared error. Our main example is that of estimating an unknown normal density. In the process of developing and discussing this somewhat counter-intuitive and half-paradoxical example we touch on several tangential issues of interest, pertaining to exact small-sample analysis of density estimators.

</details>


### [197] [Finite-sample bounds for multi-output system identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19073)
*Léo Simpson,Katrin Baumgärtner,Johannes Köhler,Moritz Diehl*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文提出了正则化线性回归的均匀时间有限样本界，适用于向量值输出和条件零均值亚高斯噪声，改进了现有结果并适用于系统辨识问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有限样本界大多针对标量输出，且对回归量与噪声间依赖关系假设较强。本文旨在建立更通用、更紧的有限样本界，适用于具有未知依赖关系的多输出回归和系统辨识问题。

Method: 重新审视经典的自归一化鞅论证方法，将其扩展到向量值输出情况。使用条件零均值亚高斯噪声假设，允许回归量与过去噪声项之间存在未知依赖关系。

Result: 获得了比现有技术更通用、更紧的有限样本界，可直接应用于多输出回归。这些界适用于具有反馈机制或系统动力学诱导的依赖关系的情况，可应用于仿射参数系统辨识问题。

Conclusion: 提出的有限样本界在通用性和紧致性方面优于现有结果，特别适用于系统辨识和安全关键应用中的随机学习控制器设计，可能带来显著改进。

Abstract: This paper presents uniform-in-time finite-sample bounds for regularized linear regression with vector-valued outputs and conditionally zero-mean subgaussian noise. By revisiting classical self-normalized martingale arguments, we obtain bounds that apply directly to multi-output regression, unlike most of the prior work. Compared to the state of the art, the new results are more general and yield tighter bounds, even for scalar-valued outputs. The mild assumptions we use allow for unknown dependencies between regressors and past noise terms, typically induced by system dynamics or feedback mechanisms. Therefore, these novel finite-sample bounds can be applied to many affine-in-parameter system identification problems, including the identification of a linear time-invariant system from full-state measurements. These new results may lead to significant improvements in stochastic learning-based controllers for safety-critical applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Frayed RoPE and Long Inputs: A Geometric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18017)
*Davis Wertheimer,Aozhong Zhang,Derrick Liu,Penghang Yin,Naigang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoPE-ID：通过高频旋转部分通道来修复RoPE在长序列上的性能崩溃问题，保持注意力机制中的关键/查询聚类分离


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE在输入长度超过训练长度时会导致性能崩溃，现有分析仅指出通道旋转"超出分布"，但未阐明额外旋转如何导致病理行为。需要从几何角度理解RoPE注意力机制，并提出解决方案。

Method: 通过经验与理论分析建立RoPE注意力的统一几何理解，发现注意力诱导关键/查询潜在点云的紧密聚类，形成sink tokens。提出RoPE-ID：对部分通道应用高频旋转，保持关键/查询聚类分离，使注意力层能够泛化到更长输入。

Result: 在1B和3B参数Transformer上使用LongBench和RULER信息检索基准测试，证明了RoPE-ID对扩展输入的有效性。

Conclusion: RoPE在长输入上的病理行为源于破坏了关键/查询聚类分离，抑制了sink tokens功能。RoPE-ID通过简单修改解决了这一问题，使模型能够处理超出训练长度的输入。

Abstract: Rotary Positional Embedding (RoPE) is a widely adopted technique for encoding position in language models, which, while effective, causes performance breakdown when input length exceeds training length. Prior analyses assert (rightly) that long inputs cause channels to rotate ``out of distribution,'' but it is not clear how extra rotation relates to or causes pathological behavior. Through empirical and theoretical analysis we advance a unified geometric understanding of attention behavior with RoPE. We find that attention induces tight clustering of separated key and query latent point clouds, allowing for creation of sink tokens: placeholders that allow attention heads to avoid token mixing when not required. RoPE applied to longer inputs damages this key/query cluster separation, producing pathological behavior by inhibiting sink token functionality. From this geometric perspective, we propose RoPE-ID (In Distribution), a straightforward modification that allows attention layers to generalize to longer inputs out of the box: apply RoPE with high frequency to a subset of channels. We demonstrate the effectiveness of RoPE-ID for extended inputs using 1B and 3B parameter Transformers on the LongBench and RULER information retrieval benchmarks.

</details>


### [199] [Engineering Verifiable Modularity in Transformers via Per-Layer Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18029)
*J. Clayton Kerce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过架构干预（双流处理、逐层监督、门控注意力）来暴露Transformer的隐藏模块性，使模型对特定组件的消融产生可预测的行为变化，将可解释性从被动观察转变为主动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型存在"九头蛇效应"——分布式冗余使得即使消融关键注意力头也只会产生最小行为变化，导致基于相关性的可解释性方法无法实现真正的因果预测和控制。

Method: 提出三种架构干预：1) 双流处理分离token和上下文表示；2) 逐层监督为每个深度提供独立梯度信号；3) 门控注意力正则化趋向离散激活模式。使用逐层监督训练模型。

Result: 使用逐层监督训练的模型消融效果比架构相同的标准训练模型大5-23倍，控制杠杆提高4倍。消融影响的标准差从0.63%增加到6.32%，揭示了哪些预测依赖于哪些电路，而非测量噪声。

Conclusion: 架构干预（特别是逐层监督）能够暴露Transformer的隐藏模块性，使消融产生可预测的行为变化，为将可解释性从被动观察转变为主动控制提供了方法论基础。

Abstract: Transformers resist surgical control. Ablating an attention head identified as critical for capitalization produces minimal behavioral change because distributed redundancy compensates for damage. This Hydra effect renders interpretability illusory: we may identify components through correlation, but cannot predict or control their causal role. We demonstrate that architectural interventions can expose hidden modularity. Our approach combines dual-stream processing separating token and contextual representations, per-layer supervision providing independent gradient signal at each depth, and gated attention regularizing toward discrete activation patterns. When trained with per-layer supervision, models produce ablation effects 5 to 23 times larger than architecturally identical controls trained with standard objectives. This enables 4 times greater control leverage on targeted behaviors: scaling identified attention heads produces smooth, predictable changes in model output. The key finding is architectural. Without per-layer supervision, ablation damage concentrates near zero with low variance (Winograd standard deviation 0.63%). With per-layer supervision, effects spread widely (standard deviation 6.32%), revealing which predictions depend on which circuits. The larger variance is not measurement noise but the signature of unmasked modularity. We validate our approach through three components: engineered features that capture computational dynamics rather than vocabulary structure (validated by near-zero correlation with raw activation clustering), an architecture providing positive control for modularity, and causal experiments demonstrating functional reorganization where different tasks route through different attention heads. This es tablishes a methodology for transforming interpretability from passive observation to active control.

</details>


### [200] [Towards Differentiating Between Failures and Domain Shifts in Industrial Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18032)
*Natalia Wojak-Strzelecka,Szymon Bobek,Grzegorz J. Nalepa,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种能够区分故障与正常域偏移的异常检测方法，结合变更点检测、域适应和可解释AI，应用于钢铁工厂数据流


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异常检测方法常将数据分布的正常变化（如处理新产品导致的域偏移）误判为故障，这在实际应用中降低了系统的实用性。需要区分真正的故障与系统正常运行中的健康变化。

Method: 1) 使用改进的Page-Hinkley变更点检测器识别域偏移和潜在故障；2) 结合监督域适应算法进行快速在线异常检测；3) 集成可解释人工智能(XAI)组件帮助操作人员最终区分域偏移与故障

Result: 方法在钢铁工厂数据流实验中进行了验证，能够有效区分故障与正常域偏移，提高异常检测的准确性和实用性

Conclusion: 提出的综合方法不仅检测数据分布变化和异常，还能区分故障与正常域偏移，增强了工业系统的实际鲁棒性，通过XAI组件提供可解释性支持操作人员决策

Abstract: Anomaly and failure detection methods are crucial in identifying deviations from normal system operational conditions, which allows for actions to be taken in advance, usually preventing more serious damages. Long-lasting deviations indicate failures, while sudden, isolated changes in the data indicate anomalies. However, in many practical applications, changes in the data do not always represent abnormal system states. Such changes may be recognized incorrectly as failures, while being a normal evolution of the system, e.g. referring to characteristics of starting the processing of a new product, i.e. realizing a domain shift. Therefore, distinguishing between failures and such ''healthy'' changes in data distribution is critical to ensure the practical robustness of the system. In this paper, we propose a method that not only detects changes in the data distribution and anomalies but also allows us to distinguish between failures and normal domain shifts inherent to a given process. The proposed method consists of a modified Page-Hinkley changepoint detector for identification of the domain shift and possible failures and supervised domain-adaptation-based algorithms for fast, online anomaly detection. These two are coupled with an explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) component that aims at helping the human operator to finally differentiate between domain shifts and failures. The method is illustrated by an experiment on a data stream from the steel factory.

</details>


### [201] [Taming Epilepsy: Mean Field Control of Whole-Brain Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18035)
*Ming Li,Ting Gao,Jingqiao Dua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GK-MFG框架，结合储层计算和APAC-Net，通过图正则化Koopman算子嵌入EEG动态到线性空间，实现癫痫发作抑制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫发作期间的高维神经动力学控制面临非线性特性和复杂脑连接的挑战，需要一种既能有效抑制发作又尊重大脑功能拓扑结构的方法

Method: 提出Graph-Regularized Koopman Mean-Field Game (GK-MFG)框架：1) 使用Reservoir Computing近似Koopman算子，将EEG动态嵌入线性潜空间；2) 采用Alternating Population and Agent Control Network (APAC-Net)解决分布控制问题；3) 基于Phase Locking Value (PLV)推导图拉普拉斯约束，施加功能拓扑结构正则化

Result: 方法实现了稳健的癫痫发作抑制，同时尊重大脑的功能拓扑结构

Conclusion: GK-MFG框架通过结合Koopman算子线性化、图正则化和平均场博弈，为癫痫发作控制提供了一种有效且尊重脑功能结构的新方法

Abstract: Controlling the high-dimensional neural dynamics during epileptic seizures remains a significant challenge due to the nonlinear characteristics and complex connectivity of the brain. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, namely Graph-Regularized Koopman Mean-Field Game (GK-MFG), which integrates Reservoir Computing (RC) for Koopman operator approximation with Alternating Population and Agent Control Network (APAC-Net) for solving distributional control problems. By embedding Electroencephalogram (EEG) dynamics into a linear latent space and imposing graph Laplacian constraints derived from the Phase Locking Value (PLV), our method achieves robust seizure suppression while respecting the functional topological structure of the brain.

</details>


### [202] [MST-Direct: Matching via Sinkhorn Transport for Multivariate Geostatistical Simulation with Complex Non-Linear Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18036)
*Tchalies Bachmann Schmitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MST-Direct算法，基于最优传输理论，使用Sinkhorn算法直接匹配多元分布并保持空间相关性结构，克服传统方法对线性相关结构的假设限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元地质统计模拟需要忠实再现地质变量间复杂的非线性依赖关系，包括双峰分布、阶跃函数和异方差关系。传统方法如高斯Copula和LU分解假设线性相关结构，通常无法保持这些复杂的联合分布模式。

Method: 提出MST-Direct算法，基于最优传输理论，使用Sinkhorn算法直接匹配多元分布同时保持空间相关性结构。该方法将所有变量作为单个多维向量同时处理，能够在整个联合空间中进行关系匹配，而不是依赖成对的线性依赖关系。

Result: 该方法能够处理复杂的非线性依赖关系，包括双峰分布、阶跃函数和异方差关系，克服了传统方法对线性相关结构的假设限制。

Conclusion: MST-Direct为多元地质统计模拟提供了一种新方法，能够更好地保持复杂的地质变量间依赖关系，相比传统线性方法具有显著优势。

Abstract: Multivariate geostatistical simulation requires the faithful reproduction of complex non-linear dependencies among geological variables, including bimodal distributions, step functions, and heteroscedastic relationships. Traditional methods such as the Gaussian Copula and LU Decomposition assume linear correlation structures and often fail to preserve these complex joint distribution patterns. We propose MST-Direct (Matching via Sinkhorn Transport), a novel algorithm based on Optimal Transport theory that uses the Sinkhorn algorithm to directly match multivariate distributions while preserving spatial correlation structures. The method processes all variables simultaneously as a single multidimensional vector, enabling relational matching across the full joint space rather than relying on pairwise linear dependencies.

</details>


### [203] [Quotient Geometry and Persistence-Stable Metrics for Swarm Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18041)
*Mark M. Bailey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了用于多智能体系统配置比较和监测的持久性稳定、对称性不变的几何表示方法，通过引入商形成空间和形成匹配度量，为群集和星座重构提供理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群集和星座重构可视为无序点配置在环境空间中的运动。现有方法缺乏能够同时处理智能体重新标记和环境对称性的几何表示，需要一种既能保持持久性稳定性又能捕捉对称不变性的度量框架来有效比较和监测多智能体配置。

Method: 引入商形成空间 $\mathcal{S}_n(M,G)=M^n/(G\times S_n)$ 和形成匹配度量 $d_{M,G}$，该度量通过优化环境对称性 $g\in G$ 和重新标记 $σ\in S_n$ 下的最坏情况分配误差获得。该方法将Vietoris-Rips持久性与该度量结合，产生持久性稳定的签名。分析了该度量空间的几何性质，包括紧致性、完备性和测地性，并研究了签名的表达能力。

Result: 证明了形成匹配度量是Gromov-Hausdorff距离的结构化松弛，满足 $d_{\mathrm{GH}}(X_x,X_y)\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$。持久性稳定性表明 $d_B(Φ_k([x]),Φ_k([y]))\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$。在紧致/完备性假设下，商空间具有相应性质；当M是测地空间时，商空间也是测地的，并沿碰撞和对称层呈现分层奇异性。在相位圆模型中证明了条件逆定理：在半圆支持和间隙标记边距下，$H_0$签名与$d_{M,G}$局部双Lipschitz等价。

Conclusion: 该框架为多智能体配置比较提供了理论严谨且计算可行的工具，将持久同伦与对称不变度量相结合，为卫星星座和群集形成等应用场景提供了有效的监测和比较方法。分层几何结构揭示了与经典配置空间的联系，而表达能力分析则明确了非单射性的机制。

Abstract: Swarm and constellation reconfiguration can be viewed as motion of an unordered point configuration in an ambient space. Here, we provide persistence-stable, symmetry-invariant geometric representations for comparing and monitoring multi-agent configuration data. We introduce a quotient formation space $\mathcal{S}_n(M,G)=M^n/(G\times S_n)$ and a formation matching metric $d_{M,G}$ obtained by optimizing a worst-case assignment error over ambient symmetries $g\in G$ and relabelings $σ\in S_n$. This metric is a structured, physically interpretable relaxation of Gromov--Hausdorff distance: the induced inter-agent metric spaces satisfy $d_{\mathrm{GH}}(X_x,X_y)\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$. Composing this bound with stability of Vietoris--Rips persistence yields $d_B(Φ_k([x]),Φ_k([y]))\le d_{M,G}([x],[y])$, providing persistence-stable signatures for reconfiguration monitoring. We analyze the metric geometry of $(\mathcal{S}_n(M,G),d_{M,G})$: under compactness/completeness assumptions on $M$ and compact $G$ it is compact/complete and the metric induces the quotient topology; if $M$ is geodesic then the quotient is geodesic and exhibits stratified singularities along collision and symmetry strata, relating it to classical configuration spaces. We study expressivity of the signatures, identifying symmetry-mismatch and persistence-compression mechanisms for non-injectivity. Finally, in a phase-circle model we prove a conditional inverse theorem: under semicircle support and a gap-labeling margin, the $H_0$ signature is locally bi-Lipschitz to $d_{M,G}$ up to an explicit factor, yielding two-sided control. Examples on $\mathbb{S}^2$ and $\mathbb{T}^m$ illustrate satellite-constellation and formation settings.

</details>


### [204] [NANOZK: Layerwise Zero-Knowledge Proofs for Verifiable Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18046)
*Zhaohui Geoffrey Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: METHOD是一个零知识证明系统，用于验证LLM推理过程，确保用户获得的结果确实来自特定模型的计算，防止服务提供商替换廉价模型或返回缓存响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用户查询专有LLM API时，无法获得加密保证确认输出确实来自所声称的模型。服务提供商可能替换廉价模型、应用激进量化或返回缓存响应，而用户支付高价却无法检测这些行为。

Method: 利用transformer推理自然分解为独立层计算的特点，开发层间证明框架，每层生成恒定大小证明。采用查找表近似处理非算术运算（softmax、GELU、LayerNorm），并引入Fisher信息引导验证机制处理无法证明所有层的场景。

Result: 在d=128的transformer模型上，METHOD生成5.5KB的恒定大小层证明（2.1KB注意力+3.5KB MLP），验证时间24ms。相比EZKL，证明大小减少70倍，证明时间快5.7倍，同时保持形式正确性保证（ε < 1e-37）。查找表近似完全保持模型困惑度。

Conclusion: METHOD通过层间分解和查找表近似实现了可扩展的LLM推理验证，为LLM API提供了加密保证，防止服务提供商的不当替代行为，同时保持模型性能不变。

Abstract: When users query proprietary LLM APIs, they receive outputs with no cryptographic assurance that the claimed model was actually used. Service providers could substitute cheaper models, apply aggressive quantization, or return cached responses - all undetectable by users paying premium prices for frontier capabilities. We present METHOD, a zero-knowledge proof system that makes LLM inference verifiable: users can cryptographically confirm that outputs correspond to the computation of a specific model.
  Our approach exploits the fact that transformer inference naturally decomposes into independent layer computations, enabling a layerwise proof framework where each layer generates a constant-size proof regardless of model width. This decomposition sidesteps the scalability barrier facing monolithic approaches and enables parallel proving. We develop lookup table approximations for non-arithmetic operations (softmax, GELU, LayerNorm) that introduce zero measurable accuracy loss, and introduce Fisher information-guided verification for scenarios where proving all layers is impractical.
  On transformer models up to d=128, METHOD generates constant-size layer proofs of 5.5KB (2.1KB attention + 3.5KB MLP) with 24 ms verification time. Compared to EZKL, METHOD achieves 70x smaller proofs and 5.7x faster proving time at d=128, while maintaining formal soundness guarantees (epsilon < 1e-37). Lookup approximations preserve model perplexity exactly, enabling verification without quality compromise.

</details>


### [205] [Variational Phasor Circuits for Phase-Native Brain-Computer Interface Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18078)
*Dibakar Sigdel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VPC是一种在S¹单位圆流形上运行的确定性经典学习架构，通过相位偏移、局部酉混合和结构化干涉实现分类，参数效率优于传统欧几里得基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受变分量子电路启发，旨在开发一种在连续单位圆流形上运行的确定性经典学习架构，为空间分布信号的分类提供统一方法，并探索相位干涉作为密集神经计算的替代方案。

Method: VPC使用可训练相位偏移替代密集实值权重矩阵，结合局部酉混合和复空间中的结构化干涉。单VPC块支持紧凑的基于相位的决策边界，堆叠VPC组合通过块间回拉归一化扩展到更深层电路。

Result: 在合成脑机接口基准测试中，VPC能够以竞争性精度解码困难的思维状态分类任务，且所需可训练参数数量显著少于标准欧几里得基线模型。

Conclusion: 单位圆相位干涉可作为密集神经计算的实际且数学原理清晰的替代方案，VPC既可作为独立分类器，也可作为未来混合相位-量子系统的前端编码层。

Abstract: We present the \textbf{Variational Phasor Circuit (VPC)}, a deterministic classical learning architecture operating on the continuous $S^1$ unit circle manifold. Inspired by variational quantum circuits, VPC replaces dense real-valued weight matrices with trainable phase shifts, local unitary mixing, and structured interference in the ambient complex space. This phase-native design provides a unified method for both binary and multi-class classification of spatially distributed signals. A single VPC block supports compact phase-based decision boundaries, while stacked VPC compositions extend the model to deeper circuits through inter-block pull-back normalization. Using synthetic brain-computer interface benchmarks, we show that VPC can decode difficult mental-state classification tasks with competitive accuracy and substantially fewer trainable parameters than standard Euclidean baselines. These results position unit-circle phase interference as a practical and mathematically principled alternative to dense neural computation, and motivate VPC as both a standalone classifier and a front-end encoding layer for future hybrid phasor-quantum systems.

</details>


### [206] [SLEA-RL: Step-Level Experience Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Agentic Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18079)
*Prince Zizhuang Wang,Shuli Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLEA-RL：一种在每一步决策时基于当前观察检索相关经验的强化学习框架，解决多轮工具使用任务中静态经验检索不匹配的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在训练时孤立操作，无法利用跨回合积累的经验。现有经验增强方法仅基于初始任务描述检索一次经验并在整个回合中保持不变，但在多轮设置中，随着回合进展，观察不断变化，这种静态检索会变得越来越不匹配。

Method: SLEA-RL包含三个组件：(1) 步骤级观察聚类：将结构等价的环境状态分组，实现高效的基于聚类的检索；(2) 自演化经验库：通过基于分数的准入和速率限制提取来提炼成功策略和失败模式；(3) 策略优化：通过步骤级信用分配在多轮回合中进行细粒度优势估计。经验库通过语义分析而非梯度更新与策略共同演化。

Result: 在长视野多轮智能体基准测试中，SLEA-RL相比各种强化学习基线实现了更优的性能。

Conclusion: SLEA-RL通过步骤级经验检索和自演化经验库，有效解决了多轮工具使用任务中静态经验检索的局限性，显著提升了LLM智能体的性能。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown strong results on multi-turn tool-use tasks, yet they operate in isolation during training, failing to leverage experiences accumulated across episodes. Existing experience-augmented methods address this by organizing trajectories into retrievable libraries, but they retrieve experiences only once based on the initial task description and hold them constant throughout the episode. In multi-turn settings where observations change at every step, this static retrieval becomes increasingly mismatched as episodes progress. We propose SLEA-RL (Step-Level Experience-Augmented Reinforcement Learning), a framework that retrieves relevant experiences at each decision step conditioned on the current observation. SLEA-RL operates through three components: (i) step-level observation clustering that groups structurally equivalent environmental states for efficient cluster-indexed retrieval; (ii) a self-evolving experience library that distills successful strategies and failure patterns through score-based admission and rate-limited extraction; and (iii) policy optimization with step-level credit assignment for fine-grained advantage estimation across multi-turn episodes. The experience library evolves alongside the policy through semantic analysis rather than gradient updates. Experiments on long-horizon multi-turn agent benchmarks demonstrate that SLEA-RL achieves superior performance compared to various reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [207] [Enhancing Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuning with an Online Refiner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18088)
*Hao Ma,Zhiqiang Pu,Yang Liu,Xiaolin Ai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态约束方法解决强化学习微调中约束与优化目标的冲突，通过参考模型作为在线精炼器生成最小修正输出，实现约束强度随模型能力自适应调整


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束在稳定强化学习微调的同时限制了模型发现更好解决方案的能力，存在约束强度与优化目标之间的固有冲突

Method: 使用参考模型作为在线精炼器，接收微调模型的输出并生成最小修正版本，保留正确内容的同时修正错误，然后通过监督微调损失训练模型产生精炼输出

Result: 在对话和代码生成任务上的实验表明，动态约束方法优于KL正则化和无约束基线，在保持训练稳定性的同时获得显著更高的任务奖励

Conclusion: 动态约束通过自适应调整约束强度解决了约束与优化目标的冲突，实现了更优的性能与稳定性平衡

Abstract: Constraints are essential for stabilizing reinforcement learning fine-tuning (RFT) and preventing degenerate outputs, yet they inherently conflict with the optimization objective because stronger constraints limit the ability of a fine-tuned model to discover better solutions. We propose \textit{dynamic constraints} that resolve this tension by adapting to the evolving capabilities of the fine-tuned model based on the insight that constraints should only intervene when degenerate outputs occur. We implement this by using a reference model as an \textit{online refiner} that takes the response from the fine-tuned model and generates a minimally corrected version which preserves correct content verbatim while fixing errors. A supervised fine-tuning loss then trains the fine-tuned model to produce the refined output. This mechanism yields a constraint that automatically strengthens or relaxes based on output quality. Experiments on dialogue and code generation show that dynamic constraints outperform both KL regularization and unconstrained baselines, achieving substantially higher task rewards while maintaining training stability.

</details>


### [208] [ARTEMIS: A Neuro Symbolic Framework for Economically Constrained Market Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18107)
*Rahul D Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARTEMIS是一个神经符号框架，通过结合拉普拉斯神经算子编码器、神经随机微分方程和可微符号瓶颈，在量化金融中实现无套利约束和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化金融中的深度学习模型通常作为黑箱运行，缺乏可解释性，并且未能纳入无套利约束等基本经济原理。

Method: ARTEMIS框架包含三个核心组件：连续时间拉普拉斯神经算子编码器、通过物理信息损失正则化的神经随机微分方程，以及蒸馏可解释交易规则的可微符号瓶颈。该模型通过两个新颖的正则化项强制执行经济合理性：惩罚局部无套利违反的Feynman-Kac PDE残差，以及限制瞬时夏普比率的市场风险价格惩罚。

Result: 在四个数据集（Jane Street、Optiver、Time-IMM和DSLOB）上评估ARTEMIS，结果显示在DSLOB上达到64.96%的方向准确率，在Time-IMM上达到96.0%，均优于六个强基线模型。消融研究表明，移除PDE损失会使方向准确率从64.89%降至50.32%。

Conclusion: ARTEMIS通过提供可解释的、基于经济原理的预测，弥合了深度学习能力与量化金融所需透明度之间的差距。

Abstract: Deep learning models in quantitative finance often operate as black boxes, lacking interpretability and failing to incorporate fundamental economic principles such as no-arbitrage constraints. This paper introduces ARTEMIS (Arbitrage-free Representation Through Economic Models and Interpretable Symbolics), a novel neuro-symbolic framework combining a continuous-time Laplace Neural Operator encoder, a neural stochastic differential equation regularised by physics-informed losses, and a differentiable symbolic bottleneck that distils interpretable trading rules. The model enforces economic plausibility via two novel regularisation terms: a Feynman-Kac PDE residual penalising local no-arbitrage violations, and a market price of risk penalty bounding the instantaneous Sharpe ratio. We evaluate ARTEMIS against six strong baselines on four datasets: Jane Street, Optiver, Time-IMM, and DSLOB (a synthetic crash regime). Results demonstrate ARTEMIS achieves state-of-the-art directional accuracy, outperforming all baselines on DSLOB (64.96%) and Time-IMM (96.0%). A comprehensive ablation study confirms each component's contribution: removing the PDE loss reduces directional accuracy from 64.89% to 50.32%. Underperformance on Optiver is attributed to its long sequence length and volatility-focused target. By providing interpretable, economically grounded predictions, ARTEMIS bridges the gap between deep learning's power and the transparency demanded in quantitative finance.

</details>


### [209] [Tula: Optimizing Time, Cost, and Generalization in Distributed Large-Batch Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18112)
*Sahil Tyagi,Feiyi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tula是一个自动优化卷积模型大批量训练时间、成本和收敛质量的服务，通过结合并行系统建模和统计性能预测来寻找最优批量大小，实现训练加速和泛化性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式训练通过横向扩展（增加节点）或纵向扩展（增大批量大小）来提高每轮迭代处理的批次数量，但最大配置不一定带来最佳性能。横向扩展引入额外通信开销，纵向扩展受计算成本和设备内存限制。简单增加批量大小会导致收益递减：训练时间和成本最初减少但最终趋于平稳，在时间/成本与批量大小的帕累托曲线上形成拐点。最优批量大小取决于底层模型、数据和可用计算资源。大批量训练还存在泛化差距导致模型质量下降的问题。

Method: Tula结合并行系统建模与统计性能预测，自动优化卷积模型大批量训练的时间、成本和收敛质量。它通过建模分析来识别最优批量大小，能够预测训练时间和成本，并成功缓解泛化差距问题。

Result: Tula在多个模型上预测训练时间和成本的误差在7.5-14%范围内，相比标准大批量训练实现了高达20倍的整体加速，并在各种视觉任务上平均提高测试准确率9%，成功缓解了泛化差距并同时加速训练。

Conclusion: Tula通过自动优化大批量训练配置，有效解决了分布式训练中批量大小选择的权衡问题，在加速训练的同时改善了模型泛化性能，为卷积模型的大批量训练提供了实用的优化解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed training increases the number of batches processed per iteration either by scaling-out (adding more nodes) or scaling-up (increasing the batch-size). However, the largest configuration does not necessarily yield the best performance. Horizontal scaling introduces additional communication overhead, while vertical scaling is constrained by computation cost and device memory limits. Thus, simply increasing the batch-size leads to diminishing returns: training time and cost decrease initially but eventually plateaus, creating a knee-point in the time/cost versus batch-size pareto curve. The optimal batch-size therefore depends on the underlying model, data and available compute resources. Large batches also suffer from worse model quality due to the well-known generalization gap. In this paper, we present Tula, an online service that automatically optimizes time, cost, and convergence quality for large-batch training of convolutional models. It combines parallel-systems modeling with statistical performance prediction to identify the optimal batch-size. Tula predicts training time and cost within 7.5-14% error across multiple models, and achieves up to 20x overall speedup and improves test accuracy by 9% on average over standard large-batch training on various vision tasks, thus successfully mitigating the generalization gap and accelerating training at the same time.

</details>


### [210] [VC-Soup: Value-Consistency Guided Multi-Value Alignment for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18113)
*Hefei Xu,Le Wu,Yu Wang,Min Hou,Han Wu,Zhen Zhang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VC-soup：基于价值一致性的数据过滤与参数合并框架，通过奖励间隙向量余弦相似度筛选高一致性偏好对，训练平滑策略模型后进行线性组合与帕累托过滤，有效缓解多价值对齐中的冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在内容生成、交互和决策中的广泛应用，使其与人类价值观对齐成为可信AI的核心目标。多价值对齐面临两大挑战：1）为每种价值组合单独训练模型成本过高；2）价值冲突严重降低对齐性能，难以实现不同人类价值观间的有利权衡。

Method: 提出VC-soup框架：1）设计基于奖励间隙向量与全1向量余弦相似度的价值一致性度量；2）过滤各价值数据集中低一致性偏好对；3）在剩余数据上训练获得平滑、价值一致且保持线性模式连接性的策略模型；4）线性组合这些策略模型并进行跨价值帕累托过滤以获得平衡的多价值性能解决方案。

Result: 大量实验和理论分析表明，VC-soup能有效缓解冲突，在多价值对齐任务中持续优于现有方法，实现了更好的跨价值权衡性能。

Conclusion: 从数据价值一致性角度重新审视多价值对齐问题，提出的VC-soup框架通过一致性度量、数据过滤和参数合并，成功解决了多价值对齐中的训练成本和冲突问题，为可信AI中的价值观对齐提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly shape content generation, interaction, and decision-making across the Web, aligning them with human values has become a central objective in trustworthy AI. This challenge becomes even more pronounced when aligning multiple, potentially conflicting human values. Although recent approaches, such as reward reweighting, prompt-based supervised fine-tuning, and model merging, attempt to tackle multi-value alignment, they still face two major limitations: (1) training separate models for each value combination is prohibitively expensive; (2) value conflicts substantially degrade alignment performance. These limitations make it difficult to achieve favorable trade-offs across diverse human values. To address these challenges, we revisit multi-value alignment from the perspective of value consistency in data and propose VC-soup, a data filtering and parameter merging framework grounded in value-consistent learning. We first design a value consistency metric based on the cosine similarity between the reward-gap vector of each preference pair and an all-ones vector, which quantifies its cross-value coherence. We then filter out low-consistency preference pairs in each value dataset and train on the remaining data to obtain smooth, value-consistent policy models that better preserve linear mode connectivity. Finally, we linearly combine these policies and apply Pareto filtering across values to obtain solutions with balanced multi-value performance. Extensive experiments and theoretical analysis demonstrate that VC-soup effectively mitigates conflicts and consistently outperforms existing multi-value alignment methods.

</details>


### [211] [LLM-Augmented Computational Phenotyping of Long Covid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18115)
*Jing Wang,Jie Shen,Amar Sra,Qiaomin Xie,Jeremy C Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Grace Cycle框架，利用LLM增强的计算表型分析方法从纵向患者数据中发现临床有意义的亚组，在13,511名长新冠患者中识别出三种不同表型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长新冠作为一种复杂且持续的状况，其临床亚表型仍知之甚少，而表型表征对于理解慢性疾病的异质性和指导个性化干预至关重要。

Method: 提出LLM增强的计算表型框架"Grace Cycle"，通过迭代整合假设生成、证据提取和特征精炼，从纵向患者数据中发现临床有意义的亚组。

Result: 在13,511名长新冠参与者中识别出三种不同的临床表型：保护型、应答型和难治型。这些表型在峰值症状严重程度、基线疾病负担和纵向剂量-反应模式上表现出明显分离，在多个独立维度上具有强统计支持。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了如何将大语言模型整合到基于原则、统计基础的管道中，用于从复杂纵向数据进行表型筛查。提出的框架是疾病无关的，为发现临床可解释的亚表型提供了通用方法。

Abstract: Phenotypic characterization is essential for understanding heterogeneity in chronic diseases and for guiding personalized interventions. Long COVID, a complex and persistent condition, yet its clinical subphenotypes remain poorly understood. In this work, we propose an LLM-augmented computational phenotyping framework ``Grace Cycle'' that iteratively integrates hypothesis generation, evidence extraction, and feature refinement to discover clinically meaningful subgroups from longitudinal patient data. The framework identifies three distinct clinical phenotypes, Protected, Responder, and Refractory, based on 13,511 Long Covid participants. These phenotypes exhibit pronounced separation in peak symptom severity, baseline disease burden, and longitudinal dose-response patterns, with strong statistical support across multiple independent dimensions.
  This study illustrates how large language models can be integrated into a principled, statistically grounded pipeline for phenotypic screening from complex longitudinal data. Note that the proposed framework is disease-agnostic and offers a general approach for discovering clinically interpretable subphenotypes.

</details>


### [212] [R2-Dreamer: Redundancy-Reduced World Models without Decoders or Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18202)
*Naoki Morihira,Amal Nahar,Kartik Bharadwaj,Yasuhiro Kato,Akinobu Hayashi,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: R2-Dreamer：一种基于冗余减少自监督目标的解码器免模型强化学习框架，无需数据增强即可防止表示崩溃，在视觉控制任务中表现优异且训练更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图像的模型强化学习中，学习能够从无关视觉细节中提取关键信息的表示是一个核心挑战。重建方法常浪费容量于任务无关区域，而解码器免方法依赖数据增强作为外部正则化器，限制了方法的通用性。

Method: 提出R2-Dreamer框架，采用基于Barlow Twins启发的冗余减少自监督目标作为内部正则化器，防止表示崩溃。该方法可轻松集成到现有框架中，无需依赖数据增强。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite和Meta-World上，R2-Dreamer与DreamerV3和TD-MPC2等强基线竞争，训练速度比DreamerV3快1.59倍。在DMC-Subtle（包含微小任务相关物体）上获得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 有效的内部正则化器能够实现通用、高性能的解码器免模型强化学习。冗余减少目标为视觉表示学习提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: A central challenge in image-based Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) is to learn representations that distill essential information from irrelevant visual details. While promising, reconstruction-based methods often waste capacity on large task-irrelevant regions. Decoder-free methods instead learn robust representations by leveraging Data Augmentation (DA), but reliance on such external regularizers limits versatility. We propose R2-Dreamer, a decoder-free MBRL framework with a self-supervised objective that serves as an internal regularizer, preventing representation collapse without resorting to DA. The core of our method is a redundancy-reduction objective inspired by Barlow Twins, which can be easily integrated into existing frameworks. On DeepMind Control Suite and Meta-World, R2-Dreamer is competitive with strong baselines such as DreamerV3 and TD-MPC2 while training 1.59x faster than DreamerV3, and yields substantial gains on DMC-Subtle with tiny task-relevant objects. These results suggest that an effective internal regularizer can enable versatile, high-performance decoder-free MBRL. Code is available at https://github.com/NM512/r2dreamer.

</details>


### [213] [Gradient-Informed Temporal Sampling Improves Rollout Accuracy in PDE Surrogate Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18237)
*Wenshuo Wang,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GITS是一种针对神经模拟器的数据采样方法，通过联合优化局部梯度和时间覆盖来平衡模型特异性和动态信息，相比均匀采样和其他基线方法能获得更低的滚动误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经模拟器训练通常使用均匀采样的数值模拟数据，但如何在有限预算下采样训练数据以最大化滚动精度仍是一个未形式化的基本问题。现有采样方法要么容易陷入局部高信息密度区域，要么保持多样性但模型特异性不足，性能往往不优于均匀采样。

Method: 提出梯度感知时间采样（GITS）方法，联合优化引导模型的局部梯度和集合级别的时间覆盖，有效平衡模型特异性和动态信息。该方法通过两个优化目标的协同作用来选择对神经模拟器最有效的训练数据。

Result: 与多种采样基线相比，GITS选择的数据在多个PDE系统、模型主干和采样比例下实现了更低的滚动误差。消融研究证明了GITS中两个优化目标的必要性和互补性。

Conclusion: GITS为神经模拟器提供了一种有效的数据采样方法，能够平衡模型特异性和动态信息。论文还分析了GITS的成功采样模式以及该方法在某些PDE系统和模型主干上失败的情况。

Abstract: Researchers train neural simulators on uniformly sampled numerical simulation data. But under the same budget, does systematically sampled data provide the most effective information? A fundamental yet unformalized problem is how to sample training data for neural simulators so as to maximize rollout accuracy. Existing data sampling methods either tend to collapse into locally high-information-density regions, or preserve diversity but remain insufficiently model-specific, often leading to performance that is no better than uniform sampling. To address this, we propose a data sampling method tailored to neural simulators, Gradient-Informed Temporal Sampling (GITS). GITS jointly optimizes pilot-model local gradients and set-level temporal coverage, thereby effectively balancing model specificity and dynamical information. Compared with multiple sampling baselines, the data selected by GITS achieves lower rollout error across multiple PDE systems, model backbones and sample ratios. Furthermore, ablation studies demonstrate the necessity and complementarity of the two optimization objectives in GITS. In addition, we analyze the successful sampling patterns of GITS as well as the typical PDE systems and model backbones on which GITS fails.

</details>


### [214] [AGRI-Fidelity: Evaluating the Reliability of Listenable Explanations for Poultry Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18247)
*Sindhuja Madabushi,Arda Dogan,Jonathan Liu,Dian Chen,Dong S. Ha,Sook Shin,Sam H. Noh,Jin-Hee Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AGRI-Fidelity框架，用于评估家禽疾病检测中可听解释的可靠性，解决现有XAI指标在模型多重性和静态伪影问题上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI指标仅衡量单个模型的忠实性，忽略了模型多重性问题（即多个近似最优分类器可能依赖不同或虚假的声学线索）。在嘈杂的农场环境中，静态伪影（如通风噪音）可能产生忠实但不可靠的解释，而基于掩蔽的指标无法惩罚冗余捷径。

Method: 提出AGRI-Fidelity框架，结合跨模型共识和循环时间置换来构建零分布并计算错误发现率（FDR）。该方法抑制静态伪影，同时保留时间局部化的生物声学标记，无需空间真实标注。

Result: 在真实和受控数据集上的实验表明，AGRI-Fidelity能够为所有数据点提供可靠性感知的区分能力，优于基于掩蔽的指标。

Conclusion: AGRI-Fidelity是一个面向可靠性的评估框架，能够有效解决家禽疾病检测中可听解释的可靠性问题，特别是在存在模型多重性和静态伪影的嘈杂农场环境中。

Abstract: Existing XAI metrics measure faithfulness for a single model, ignoring model multiplicity where near-optimal classifiers rely on different or spurious acoustic cues. In noisy farm environments, stationary artifacts such as ventilation noise can produce explanations that are faithful yet unreliable, as masking-based metrics fail to penalize redundant shortcuts. We propose AGRI-Fidelity, a reliability-oriented evaluation framework for listenable explanations in poultry disease detection without spatial ground truth. The method combines cross-model consensus with cyclic temporal permutation to construct null distributions and compute a False Discovery Rate (FDR), suppressing stationary artifacts while preserving time-localized bioacoustic markers. Across real and controlled datasets, AGRI-Fidelity effectively provides reliability-aware discrimination for all data points versus masking-based metrics.

</details>


### [215] [MolRGen: A Training and Evaluation Setting for De Novo Molecular Generation with Reasonning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18256)
*Philippe Formont,Maxime Darrin,Ismail Ben Ayed,Pablo Piantanida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolRGen：用于从头分子生成的大规模基准和数据集，旨在训练和评估基于推理的大语言模型，解决现有方法需要真实标签监督的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理的LLMs在药物发现和分子设计中的应用大多需要真实标签监督（如已知性质修饰的分子对），而在从头分子生成任务中，目标是生成优化期望得分的新分子，这种监督信息不可得。

Method: 1) 提出用于从头分子生成和性质预测的模型评估和训练设置；2) 引入新颖的多样性感知top-k评分，同时捕捉生成分子的质量和多样性；3) 使用强化学习训练24B参数的LLM进行分子生成。

Result: 建立了MolRGen基准和数据集，展示了该设置可用于训练LLMs进行分子生成，并对训练模型的性能和局限性进行了详细分析。

Conclusion: MolRGen填补了基于推理的LLMs在从头分子生成任务中的空白，提供了无需真实标签监督的训练和评估框架，并引入了同时考虑质量和多样性的评估指标。

Abstract: Recent advances in reasoning-based large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated substantial improvements in complex problem-solving tasks. Motivated by these advances, several works have explored the application of reasoning LLMs to drug discovery and molecular design. However, most existing approaches either focus on evaluation or rely on training setups that require ground-truth labels, such as molecule pairs with known property modifications. Such supervision is unavailable in \textit{de novo} molecular generation, where the objective is to generate novel molecules that optimize a desirability score without prior knowledge of high-scoring candidates. To bridge this gap, we introduce MolRGen, a large-scale benchmark and dataset for training and evaluating reasoning-based LLMs on \textit{de novo} molecular generation. Our contributions are threefold. First, we propose a setting to evaluate and train models for \textit{de novo} molecular generation and property prediction. Second, we introduce a novel diversity-aware top-$k$ score that captures both the quality and diversity of generated molecules. Third, we show our setting can be used to train LLMs for molecular generation, training a 24B LLM with reinforcement learning, and we provide a detailed analysis of its performance and limitations.

</details>


### [216] [Discovering What You Can Control: Interventional Boundary Discovery for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18257)
*Jiaxin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IBD是一种因果特征选择方法，通过干预性边界发现来识别智能体真正能影响的观测维度，避免混淆干扰因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在混淆干扰因素的情况下，传统观测统计无法可靠区分与动作相关的维度和动作真正影响的维度，这会导致RL算法性能下降。

Method: 提出干预性边界发现(IBD)方法，应用Pearl的do-operator对智能体自身动作进行干预，使用双样本检验生成观测维度的可解释二元掩码。

Result: 在12个连续控制任务中，IBD在所有干扰水平下都接近oracle性能，而观测特征选择会主动选择混淆干扰因素并丢弃真实因果维度。

Conclusion: IBD作为一种无需学习模型的预处理方法，能有效识别智能体的因果影响范围，显著提升RL算法在存在大量干扰因素时的性能。

Abstract: Selecting relevant state dimensions in the presence of confounded distractors is a causal identification problem: observational statistics alone cannot reliably distinguish dimensions that correlate with actions from those that actions cause. We formalize this as discovering the agent's Causal Sphere of Influence and propose Interventional Boundary Discovery IBD, which applies Pearl's do-operator to the agent's own actions and uses two-sample testing to produce an interpretable binary mask over observation dimensions. IBD requires no learned models and composes with any downstream RL algorithm as a preprocessing step. Across 12 continuous control settings with up to 100 distractor dimensions, we find that: (1) observational feature selection can actively select confounded distractors while discarding true causal dimensions; (2) full-state RL degrades sharply once distractors outnumber relevant features by roughly 3:1 in our benchmarks; and (3)IBD closely tracks oracle performance across all distractor levels tested, with gains transferring across SAC and TD3.

</details>


### [217] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization in Logit Space Efficiently Enhances Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18258)
*Haocheng Luo,Zehang Deng,Thanh-Toan Do,Mehrtash Harandi,Dinh Phung,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出logits-SAM方法缓解DPO训练中的squeezing效应，通过扰动输出层logits来正则化曲率，提升DPO效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO虽然简单稳定，但存在squeezing效应（似然位移），即训练过程中偏好响应的概率会意外下降，需要理解和缓解这一现象

Method: 建立logit空间坐标动态理论框架，分析负梯度更新导致残差沿高曲率方向快速扩展的机制，提出logits-SAM方法——仅扰动输出层logits的SAM变体，计算开销小

Result: 在Pythia-2.8B、Mistral-7B、Gemma-2B-IT等多个模型和数据集上的实验表明，logits-SAM能持续提升DPO效果，并能无缝集成到其他DPO变体中

Conclusion: logits-SAM通过曲率正则化有效缓解DPO的squeezing效应，计算高效且与现有DPO变体兼容，为提升语言模型对齐效果提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has emerged as a popular algorithm for aligning pretrained large language models with human preferences, owing to its simplicity and training stability. However, DPO suffers from the recently identified squeezing effect (also known as likelihood displacement), where the probability of preferred responses decreases unintentionally during training. To understand and mitigate this phenomenon, we develop a theoretical framework that models the coordinate-wise dynamics in logit space. Our analysis reveals that negative-gradient updates cause residuals to expand rapidly along high-curvature directions, which underlies the squeezing effect, whereas Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) can suppress this behavior through its curvature-regularization effect. Building on this insight, we investigate logits-SAM, a computationally efficient variant that perturbs only the output layer with negligible overhead. Extensive experiments on Pythia-2.8B, Mistral-7B, and Gemma-2B-IT across multiple datasets and benchmarks demonstrate that logits-SAM consistently improves the effectiveness of DPO and integrates seamlessly with other DPO variants. Code is available at https://github.com/RitianLuo/logits-sam-dpo.

</details>


### [218] [Enactor: From Traffic Simulators to Surrogate World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18266)
*Yash Ranjan,Rahul Sengupta,Anand Rangarajan,Sanjay Ranka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于世界模型范式的交通参与者生成模型，使用Transformer架构捕捉参与者交互和交叉口几何特征，生成物理一致的长时程轨迹，并在仿真闭环测试中优于基线方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通微观仿真中的行为模型过于简化，无法捕捉真实的参与者交互；深度学习方法虽然能学习交互，但无法生成物理一致的长时程轨迹，且未明确处理交通交叉口的复杂动态

Method: 基于世界模型范式，开发参与者中心的生成模型，采用Transformer架构捕捉参与者交互，同时理解交通交叉口几何特征，生成基于学习行为的物理接地轨迹；在"仿真闭环"设置中测试，使用SUMO生成初始条件后由模型控制参与者动态

Result: 模型能有效捕捉复杂参与者交互，生成长时程、物理一致的轨迹，所需训练样本显著少于传统参与者中心生成方法；在交通相关和聚合指标上优于基线，KL散度指标超过基线10倍以上

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功解决了现有方法的局限性，能够生成物理一致的长时程轨迹并有效处理交通交叉口复杂动态，为交通微观仿真提供了更真实的行为建模方法

Abstract: Traffic microsimulators are widely used to evaluate road network performance under various ``what-if" conditions. However, the behavior models controlling the actions of the actors are overly simplistic and fails to capture realistic actor-actor interactions. Deep learning-based methods have been applied to model vehicles and pedestrians as ``agents" responding to their surrounding ``environment" (including lanes, signals, and neighboring agents). Although effective in learning actor-actor interaction, these approaches fail to generate physically consistent trajectories over long time periods, and they do not explicitly address the complex dynamics that arise at traffic intersections which is a critical location in urban networks. Inspired by the World Model paradigm, we have developed an actor centric generative model using transformer-based architecture that is able to capture the actor-actor interaction, at the same time understanding the geometry to the traffic intersection to generate physically grounded trajectories that are based on learned behavior. Moreover, we test the model in a live ``simulation-in-the-loop" setting, where we generate the initial conditions of the actors using SUMO and then let the model control the dynamics of the actors. We let the simulation run for 40000 timesteps (4000 seconds), testing the performance of the model on long timerange and evaluating the trajectories on traffic engineering related metrics. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively captures complex actor-actor interactions and generates long-horizon, physically consistent trajectories, while requiring significantly fewer training samples than traditional agent-centric generative approaches. Our model is able to outperform the baseline in traffic related as well as aggregate metrics where our model beats the baseline by more than 10x on the KL-Divergence.

</details>


### [219] [Detection Is Cheap, Routing Is Learned: Why Refusal-Based Alignment Evaluation Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18280)
*Gregory N. Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出对齐评估应关注"检测-路由-生成"三阶段框架，通过中国语言模型政治审查案例研究发现：探测准确率无诊断价值，手术式消融揭示实验室特定路由机制，拒绝不再是主要审查机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐评估主要关注模型是否编码危险概念和拒绝有害请求，但忽略了对齐操作的核心层面：从概念检测到行为策略的路由机制。作者以中国语言模型的政治审查作为自然实验，研究这一被忽视的中间层

Method: 使用探测、手术式消融和行为测试三种方法，对来自五个实验室的九个开源权重模型进行系统分析。重点研究政治审查这一具体对齐机制，通过消融实验揭示路由几何结构，并比较不同模型的审查策略变化

Result: 1) 探测准确率无诊断价值：政治探测、零控制和置换基线都能达到100%准确率，只有留出类别泛化测试有效；2) 手术式消融揭示实验室特定路由：移除政治敏感方向能消除审查并恢复准确事实输出，但有一个模型因架构纠缠而产生幻觉；3) 拒绝不再是主要审查机制：在一个模型家族中，硬拒绝降至零而叙事引导达到最大，使审查对仅测试拒绝的基准不可见

Conclusion: 提出"检测-路由-生成"三阶段描述框架：模型通常保留相关知识，对齐改变的是知识的表达方式。仅审计检测或拒绝的评估会错过最直接影响行为的路由机制，需要更全面的评估方法

Abstract: Current alignment evaluation mostly measures whether models encode dangerous concepts and whether they refuse harmful requests. Both miss the layer where alignment often operates: routing from concept detection to behavioral policy. We study political censorship in Chinese-origin language models as a natural experiment, using probes, surgical ablations, and behavioral tests across nine open-weight models from five labs. Three findings follow. First, probe accuracy alone is non-diagnostic: political probes, null controls, and permutation baselines can all reach 100%, so held-out category generalization is the informative test. Second, surgical ablation reveals lab-specific routing. Removing the political-sensitivity direction eliminates censorship and restores accurate factual output in most models tested, while one model confabulates because its architecture entangles factual knowledge with the censorship mechanism. Cross-model transfer fails, indicating that routing geometry is model- and lab-specific. Third, refusal is no longer the dominant censorship mechanism. Within one model family, hard refusal falls to zero while narrative steering rises to the maximum, making censorship invisible to refusal-only benchmarks. These results support a three-stage descriptive framework: detect, route, generate. Models often retain the relevant knowledge; alignment changes how that knowledge is expressed. Evaluations that audit only detection or refusal therefore miss the routing mechanism that most directly determines behavior.

</details>


### [220] [On Additive Gaussian Processes for Wind Farm Power Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18281)
*Simon M. Brealy,Lawrence A. Bull,Daniel S. Brennan,Pauline Beltrando,Anders Sommer,Nikolaos Dervilis,Keith Worden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文采用基于群体的结构健康监测方法，使用加法高斯过程分析风电场数据，揭示涡轮机特定和农场层面的功率模型变化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于群体的结构健康监测旨在在相似机器或结构之间共享信息。本文从群体层面视角出发，探索如何利用收集的风电场数据集揭示涡轮机特定和农场层面功率模型的变化

Method: 使用加法高斯过程对风电场数据集进行分析，该方法能够分离和识别涡轮机特定特征与农场层面特征的贡献

Result: 预测结果显示了风电场发电的模式，这些模式符合直觉，能够为更明智的控制和决策提供支持

Conclusion: 加法高斯过程方法能够有效揭示风电场中涡轮机层面和农场层面的功率变化模式，为基于群体的结构健康监测提供了有价值的分析工具

Abstract: Population-based Structural Health Monitoring (PBSHM) aims to share information between similar machines or structures. This paper takes a population-level perspective, exploring the use of additive Gaussian processes to reveal variations in turbine-specific and farm-level power models over a collected wind farm dataset. The predictions illustrate patterns in wind farm power generation, which follow intuition and should enable more informed control and decision-making.

</details>


### [221] [Path-Constrained Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18297)
*Zijin Gu,Tatiana Likhomanenko,Vimal Thilak,Jason Ramapuram,Navdeep Jaitly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PathMoE通过跨层共享路由器参数来约束专家路径空间，提高稀疏混合专家模型的统计效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE路由在每层独立选择专家，产生N^L个可能的专家路径，远超典型训练集规模，导致统计效率低下，难以学习路径空间的有意义结构

Method: 提出PathMoE，在连续层之间共享路由器参数，从而约束专家路径空间，无需辅助负载平衡损失

Result: 在0.9B和16B参数模型上，PathMoE在困惑度和下游任务上相比独立路由取得一致改进；分析显示相同路径的token按语言功能自然聚类，PathMoE产生更集中的分组、更好的跨层一致性和更强的路由扰动鲁棒性

Conclusion: PathMoE为理解MoE架构提供了通过专家路径视角的新视角，通过约束路径空间提高统计效率，同时改善模型性能

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable efficient scaling by activating only a subset of parameters for each input. However, conventional MoE routing selects each layer's experts independently, creating N^L possible expert paths -- for N experts across L layers. This far exceeds typical training set sizes, leading to statistical inefficiency as the model may not learn meaningful structure over such a vast path space. To constrain it, we propose \pathmoe, which shares router parameters across consecutive layers. Experiments on 0.9B and 16B parameter models demonstrate consistent improvements on perplexity and downstream tasks over independent routing, while eliminating the need for auxiliary load balancing losses. Analysis reveals that tokens following the same path naturally cluster by linguistic function, with \pathmoe{} producing more concentrated groups, better cross-layer consistency, and greater robustness to routing perturbations. These results offer a new perspective for understanding MoE architectures through the lens of expert paths.

</details>


### [222] [Learning to Reason with Curriculum I: Provable Benefits of Autocurriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18325)
*Nived Rajaraman,Audrey Huang,Miro Dudik,Robert Schapire,Dylan J. Foster,Akshay Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明自课程学习（autocurriculum）能显著降低思维链推理模型的训练成本，在监督微调中指数级减少演示需求，在强化学习中解耦计算成本与参考模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链推理模型的训练成本极高，需要收集大量人类或合成的推理轨迹并进行强化学习微调。研究旨在探索这些成本是否可通过算法设计降低，而非固有成本。

Method: 采用自课程学习方法，让模型根据自身表现自适应选择训练重点。对于监督微调，聚焦于模型当前表现不佳的提示；对于强化学习，通过自适应数据选择降低对参考模型的依赖。方法基于经典的boosting和从反例中学习技术。

Result: 自课程学习在监督微调中指数级减少所需推理演示数量；在强化学习中，将参考模型质量要求降低为几乎独立于目标准确率的预热成本，从而显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 通过自适应数据选择的自课程学习方法能显著降低思维链推理模型的训练成本，无需对提示分布或难度做假设，为高效训练推理模型提供了新途径。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning, where language models expend additional computation by producing thinking tokens prior to final responses, has driven significant advances in model capabilities. However, training these reasoning models is extremely costly in terms of both data and compute, as it involves collecting long traces of reasoning behavior from humans or synthetic generators and further post-training the model via reinforcement learning. Are these costs fundamental, or can they be reduced through better algorithmic design? We show that autocurriculum, where the model uses its own performance to decide which problems to focus training on, provably improves upon standard training recipes for both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). For SFT, we show that autocurriculum requires exponentially fewer reasoning demonstrations than non-adaptive fine-tuning, by focusing teacher supervision on prompts where the current model struggles. For RL fine-tuning, autocurriculum decouples the computational cost from the quality of the reference model, reducing the latter to a burn-in cost that is nearly independent of the target accuracy. These improvements arise purely from adaptive data selection, drawing on classical techniques from boosting and learning from counterexamples, and requiring no assumption on the distribution or difficulty of prompts.

</details>


### [223] [Escaping Offline Pessimism: Vector-Field Reward Shaping for Safe Frontier Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18326)
*Amirhossein Roknilamouki,Arnob Ghosh,Eylem Ekici,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于向量场奖励塑形的新范式，用于引导离线强化学习策略在部署时进行持续、安全的边界探索，避免陷入退化停车行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习固有的悲观性限制了智能体在线探索和收集新数据的能力。需要一种方法让智能体在离线数据集覆盖良好且模拟器可靠建模的区域边界附近进行探索，承担可控风险——进入信息丰富但中等不确定性的状态，同时保持足够接近熟悉区域以便安全恢复。

Method: 提出向量场奖励塑形范式，包含两个互补组件：1）梯度对齐项：吸引智能体朝向目标不确定性水平；2）旋转流项：促进沿不确定性流形局部切平面的运动。该方法基于离线数据训练的不确定性预言机，旨在诱导持续的安全边界探索。

Result: 理论分析表明该奖励结构自然诱导沿边界的持续探索行为，同时防止退化解。在2D连续导航任务中，将提出的奖励塑形与Soft Actor-Critic集成，验证了智能体成功遍历不确定性边界，平衡了安全、信息丰富的数据收集与主要任务完成。
